        |  |  |        +--ro cert?                types-sros:string-not-all-spaces
        |  |  |        +--ro valid-from?          yang:date-and-time
        |  |  |        +--ro valid-to?            yang:date-and-time
        |  |  |        +--ro signature?           types-sros:string-not-all-spaces
        |  |  |        +--ro key-encryption?      types-sros:string-not-all-spaces
        |  |  |        +--ro key-size?            uint32
        |  |  |        +--ro ca-flag?             boolean
        |  |  |        +--ro subject?             types-sros:string-not-all-spaces
        |  |  |        +--ro issuer?              types-sros:string-not-all-spaces
        |  |  |        +--ro est-profile-names*   types-sros:named-item
        |  |  +--ro client-tls-profile* [client-profile-name]
        |  |  |  +--ro client-profile-name    types-sros:named-item
        |  |  |  +--ro oper-state?            types-sros:oper-state
        |  |  +--ro server-tls-profile* [server-profile-name]
        |  |  |  +--ro server-profile-name    types-sros:named-item
        |  |  |  +--ro oper-state?            types-sros:oper-state
        |  |  +--ro trust-anchor-profile* [trust-anchor-profile-name]
        |  |     +--ro trust-anchor-profile-name    types-sros:named-item
        |  |     +--ro ca-profile-down?             uint32
        |  |     +--ro trust-anchor* [ca-profile-name]
        |  |        +--ro ca-profile-name    -> ../../../../pki/ca-profile/ca-profile-name
        |  |        +--ro oper-state?        types-sros:oper-state
        |  +--ro user-params
        |     +--ro local-user
        |     |  +--ro user* [user-name]
        |     |     +--ro user-name                    types-sros:named-item
        |     |     +--ro attempted-logins?            yang:counter32
        |     |     +--ro failed-logins?               yang:counter32
        |     |     +--ro locked-out?                  boolean
        |     |     +--ro lockout-remaining-time?      types-sros:time-duration

        |     |     +--ro password-expiration-time?    yang:date-and-time
        |     |     +--ro user-ssh-public-key-count?   yang:counter32
        |     +--ro user-lockout* [user-name]

        |        +--ro remaining-login-attempts?   uint32
        |        +--ro remaining-lockout-time?     types-sros:time-duration
        +--ro time
        |  +--ro zone-type?   enumeration
        +--ro transaction
           +--ro ihub-transaction-id?   types-system:transaction-id
